Method: 针对n节点图编码为n量子比特态的情形，提供了GQML模型各组件的全面表征，构建了量子图模型设计的工具箱。该工具箱支持与经典模型自然集成、泛化已有GQML模型（在几乎零额外成本下扩展表达能力），以及直接的经典预训练策略。

Result: 提出了统一的GQML图模型设计框架，实现了与经典模型的无缝集成，泛化了现有GQML模型并在几乎零成本下提升了表达能力，同时支持直接的经典预训练策略。数值实验验证了后两种特性（泛化能力和预训练策略）的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为GQML图模型提供了系统化的设计工具箱，弥补了该领域缺乏统一理论视角的空白。该框架不仅提升了模型表达能力和实用性，还打通了经典与量子机器学习之间的桥梁，为后续GQML研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Geometric Machine Learning (GML) successes have been achieved through the thorough study and design of new equivariant neural networks. In comparison, geometric quantum machine learning (GQML) models lack such a detailed understanding and, despite already several proposals, a unifying perspective on their design remains elusive. In this work, we focus on GQML models for graph problems that showcase a lot of structure and still remain frontier in machine learning. For the case when n-node graphs are encoded in n-qubit states, we provide a comprehensive characterization of their constituents. Taken together, these furnish us with a toolbox for the design of quantum graph models, and we further probe its benefits including the natural integration with classical models, generalization of known GQML models (sometimes extending their expressivity at virtually no cost), and straightforward classical pre-training strategies. The latter two features are demonstrated in dedicated numerical experiments.

</details>


### [122] [GsOQDC: A GUI-Driven Interactive Framework for End-to-End Simulation of Optical Quantum Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00715)
*Seyed Navid Elyasi,Sima Bahrani,Rui Wang,Dimitra Simeonidou,Paolo Monti,Rui Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: GsOQDC是一个开源图形框架，整合了光网络设计、分布式量子电路编译、调度和基于离散事件仿真的远程门模拟，支持对光量子数据中心中纠缠资源动态和系统级性能进行端到端跨层评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏能够同时涵盖光网络设计、分布式量子电路编译与调度，以及系统级性能评估的集成化工具，难以对光量子数据中心（OQDC）中的纠缠资源动态和端到端性能进行跨层分析。

Method: 提出GsOQDC开源框架，集成光网络设计、分布式量子电路编译、任务调度和基于离散事件仿真（DES）的远程门模拟四大模块，支持跨层协同仿真。

Result: 实现了对光量子数据中心中纠缠资源动态变化和系统级性能的端到端跨层评估。

Conclusion: GsOQDC框架为光量子数据中心的设计与性能优化提供了统一的、可扩展的仿真与评估平台。

Abstract: We present GsOQDC, an open-source graphical framework integrating optical-network design, distributed quantum-circuit compilation, scheduling, and DES-based remote-gate simulation, enabling end-to-end cross-layer evaluation of entanglement-resource dynamics and system-level performance in Optical Quantum Data Centers.

</details>


### [123] [Conditional Enhancement of Dissipation-Induced Nonreciprocity by Quantum Squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00718)
*B. -B. Liu,D. -Y. Wang,J. Tang,Gang Chen,H. Jing,Shi-Lei Su,F. Nori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在耗散诱导非互易耦合中，通过加入χ(2)非线性引入压缩效应，发现压缩能指数级增强有效耦合，但这种增强依赖于系统配置和参数区域；同时展示了该方法在量子电池性能（存储能量、充电功率、可提取功）和光隔离器输出信号方面的显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索压缩效应对耗散诱导非互易耦合的增强作用，并研究其在不同系统配置下的表现，以提升非互易量子信息处理和非互易量子器件的性能。

Method: 在腔模、库或两者中引入χ(2)非线性来产生压缩效应；通过解析分析和参数优化研究非互易耦合的增强机制；将方法扩展到量子电池和光隔离器场景进行验证。

Result: 压缩可指数级增强有效耦合，但该增强并非普适，依赖于系统配置和参数区域；库的压缩通过耗散通道注入额外能量以非平凡方式改变动力学；量子电池的存储能量、充电功率和可提取功得到提升；光隔离器输出信号获得指数级放大。

Conclusion: 该工作为非互易量子信息处理和非互易量子器件设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the role of squeezing in improving dissipation-induced nonreciprocal coupling mediated by a common reservoir, by incorporating a $χ^{(2)}$ nonlinearity into either the cavity mode, the reservoir, or both. Squeezing is capable of exponentially enhancing the effective coupling, but this enhancement is not universal in such a nonreciprocal interaction. Instead, it depends on the system configuration and specific parameter regimes. Specifically, the squeezing of the reservoir injects additional energy into the system via the dissipation channel, modifying the underlying dynamics in a nontrivial manner. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the proposed approach enhances the performance of the quantum battery, including stored energy, charging power, and ergotropy. We provide the corresponding analytical expressions, together with a systematic analysis of energy transport and parameter optimization. Extending the framework to optical isolation, we observe an exponentially amplified output signal. Our results open a new avenue for nonreciprocal quantum information processing and nonreciprocal quantum device design.

</details>


### [124] [Experimental Quantification of Layered Error Suppression in Fiber-Interconnected Quantum Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00735)
*Seyed Navid Elyasi,Sima Bahrani,Rui Wang,Dimitra Simeonidou,Paolo Monti,Rui Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过实验量化了光纤连接超导量子处理单元（QPUs）中结合误差缓解技术的误差抑制效果，在真实噪声条件下展示了跨互连量子处理单元操作保真度超过20%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决光纤连接的超导量子处理单元在实际噪声条件下的误差问题，探索组合误差缓解技术是否能有效提升跨QPUs的操作保真度。

Method: 在光纤连接的多个超导量子处理单元上进行实验，结合多种误差缓解技术，在真实噪声条件下量化误差抑制效果。

Result: 组合误差缓解技术使跨互连量子处理单元的操作保真度提升了超过20%。

Conclusion: 组合误差缓解技术能有效抑制光纤连接超导QPUs在实际噪声条件下的误差，显著提升跨单元操作保真度。

Abstract: We perform experiments to quantify error suppression in fiber-connected superconducting QPUs using combined error mitigation techniques, demonstrating over 20\% improvement in operational fidelity across interconnected quantum processing units under realistic noise conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Asymmetric Light Scattering from an Atomic System with Gain: A Quantum Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00779)
*Lorena Acevedo,Manuel Donaire*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非相干泵浦的双原子系统中光散射的方向性，发现量子方法预测的侧向照明非互易性与经典方法结果相矛盾，这种不对称性源于原子发射光子间的干涉以及探针场的相位差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非宇称对称光学系统中增益与损耗部分补偿条件下的光散射方向性，特别是双原子系统中非相干泵浦对散射不对称性的影响。

Method: 采用全量子分析方法，研究入射光垂直于或平行于原子间轴入射时，双原子系统辐射散射的方向性。分别考虑正面照明和侧面照明两种情况。

Result: 不对称程度取决于泵浦速率，而发射的优先方向取决于原子间距离和探针场相对于共振频率的失谐。正面照明时，不对称性源于不同原子发射光子的干涉；侧面照明时，不对称性源于探针场光子到达每个原子的相位差与原子间发射光子的干涉共同作用。量子方法发现侧面照明时前向散射功率取决于入射侧，揭示了系统量子光学响应的非互易性，这与经典方法结果相矛盾。

Conclusion: 该系统在量子光学层面表现出非互易性，经典方法无法捕捉到这一效应，表明在处理这类部分增益-损耗补偿系统时需采用量子方法。

Abstract: We study the the scattering of light by a binary system of identical atoms in which one of them is incoherently pumped. This system belongs to the kind of non-parity symmetric optical systems in which gains and losses are partially compensated. We carry out a fully quantum analysis of the directionality of the radiation scattered from the atoms when the incident light strikes the system either perpendicular or alongside the interatomic axis. We find that, generally, while the degree of asymmetry depends on the pump rate, the preferred direction for emission depends on the interatomic distance and the detuning of the probe field with respect to the resonant frequency. On physical grounds, for the case of frontal illumination, the asymmetry is the result of the interference of the photons emitted from different atoms. On the contrary, for side lighting, the asymmetry with respect to the side of incidence is caused by both the phase difference between the probe field photons that strike each atom and the interference between the photons emitted from different atoms. Further, for side lighting too, our quantum approach demonstrates that the forward scattered power depends on the side of incidence, which reveals the lack of reciprocity in the quantum optical response of the system. This result conflicts with what is obtained within a classical approach.

</details>


### [126] [Bias-Preserving Gates and Quantum Error Correction With Dual-Rail Cat Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00786)
*Debjyoti Biswas,Nikhil Sharma,Alberto Salvador,Rui Wang,Mats Granath,Adithi Udupa,Giulia Ferrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双轨猫码(DRCC)，一种将内层猫码与外层双轨结构级联的玻色子编码，通过继承并增强两种编码的优势，实现硬件高效、偏倚保持和擦除鲁棒的容错量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双轨码可高效检测单光子损耗（转化为擦除错误），猫码提供资源高效、偏倚定制的纠错方案和偏倚保持的逻辑门操作，但两者单独使用均存在局限性。结合二者优势可克服各自不足。

Method: 将内层猫码与外层双轨结构级联构成DRCC；通过与外层重复码级联实现确定性单光子损耗纠错；利用编码固有噪声偏倚，仅用分束器相互作用构建通用逻辑门集。

Result: 所有逻辑操作保持擦除偏倚噪声结构；实现无相对几何相位、确定性擦除检测与纠错、稳定化与综合征同时提取等优势。

Conclusion: DRCC是一种有前景的玻色子编码，兼具硬件高效、偏倚保持和擦除鲁棒的容错量子计算特性。

Abstract: Scalable fault-tolerant quantum computation requires quantum error-correcting codes that simultaneously support universal logical operations, suppress hardware-specific noise, and enable efficient handling of photon-loss errors. Bosonic encodings such as the dual-rail and cat codes each offer attractive features but also exhibit important limitations when used in isolation. The dual-rail code enables efficient single-photon-loss detection by converting leakage out of the computational subspace induced by photon-loss errors into an erasure error. In contrast, the cat code provides a resource-efficient, bias-tailored error-correction scheme with bias-preserving logical gate operations. Here, we introduce the dual-rail cat code (DRCC), a concatenated bosonic encoding that combines an inner cat code with an outer dual-rail structure, thereby inheriting and enhancing the advantages of both constituent codes. We analyse the error-correction properties of the DRCC and propose a deterministic single-photon-loss correction protocol by concatenating it with an outer repetition code. Exploiting the code's intrinsic noise bias, we construct a universal set of logical gates using only beam-splitter interactions and demonstrate that all logical operations preserve the erasure-biased noise structure. The DRCC offers several distinctive advantages, including the absence of relative geometric phases during gate operations, deterministic erasure detection and correction, and simultaneous syndrome extraction without interrupting stabilisation. These features make the DRCC a promising bosonic code for hardware-efficient, bias-preserving, and erasure-resilient fault-tolerant quantum computation.

</details>


### [127] [Hierarchy of hidden nonlocality: A genuine activation of Incompletability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00797)
*Soumajit Das,Shampa Mondal,Preeti Parashar,Atanu Bhunia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了量子非局域性中“不完全性激活”的概念，证明存在初始可被LOCC完美区分且无局部冗余的正交集，可在LOCC下转化为严格不完全集。不完全性激活必然导致非局域性激活，但反之不成立。在LICC框架下，任何可激活不完全性的集合仍可扩展为完备正交基，但该基不再能被LOCC完美区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究局部状态区分中“不完全性”的作用，揭示局部可区分性与非局域性之间的深层层次关系，并理解相干性、不完全性与非局域性之间的基本相互作用。

Method: 通过构造具体量子态集合，分析其在LOCC和LICC操作下的区分性变化，定义不完全性激活的概念，并建立不完全性激活与非局域性激活之间的层次关系。

Result: 发现了初始可完美区分且无局部冗余的正交集能在LOCC下转变为严格不完全集；证明了不完全性激活必然蕴含非局域性激活，但反之不成立；在LICC框架下，可激活不完全性的集合仍可扩展为完备正交基，但完成后的基不再LOCC可区分。

Conclusion: 揭示了局部可区分性、不完全性、相干性和非局域性之间的基本相互作用，为理解局部可访问量子信息的结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Quantum nonlocality admits several operational manifestations, one of which emerges from sets of orthogonal quantum states that cannot be perfectly distinguished by local operations and classical communication (LOCC). Such sets are regarded as nonlocal because their perfect discrimination requires global measurements. In contrast, sets that are perfectly distinguishable by LOCC are generally considered locally accessible and operationally classical. In this work, we investigate the role of incompletability in local state discrimination and introduce the notion of \emph{activation of incompletability}. Specifically, we demonstrate the existence of orthogonal sets that are initially perfectly distinguishable by LOCC and free from local redundancy, but which can be transformed via LOCC into strictly incompletable sets. We prove that activation of incompletability necessarily implies activation of nonlocality, whereas the converse fails in general, thereby establishing a hierarchy between the two activation phenomena. Furthermore, within the framework of local incoherent operations and classical communication (LICC), we show that any set whose incompletability can be activated can nevertheless be extended to a complete orthonormal basis of the Hilbert space, although the resulting completed basis is no longer perfectly distinguishable by LOCC. Our results uncover a fundamental interplay among local distinguishability, incompletability, coherence, and nonlocality, and provide new insight into the structure of locally accessible quantum information.

</details>


### [128] [Synthesizing Compound Pulse Gadgets for Hamiltonian Simulation on Trapped-Ion Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00826)
*Ria Patel,Masoud Hakimi Heris,Yuan Liu,Frank Mueller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跳过量级门拼接的 holistic 脉冲合成策略，直接将 QSVT 算法编译为连续复合脉冲小工具，在捕获离子硬件上实现 Hamiltonian 模拟，利用 GRAPE 生成复合脉冲，通过 Lindblad 主方程仿真验证，结果显示显著压缩脉冲时序并消除控制层延迟，有望在 T₂ 退相干极限内实现更深计算深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准门级编译将量子操作视为离散单元，导致物理控制层执行高度碎片化的激光脉冲，硬件与软件之间存在严重脱节，引入显著的物理噪声和开销，限制了高精度量子算法（如 QSVT）在近中期捕获离子硬件上的执行效率。

Method: 提出 holistic 脉冲合成策略，跳过量级门拼接，直接将 QSVT 算法编译为连续复合脉冲小工具（compound pulse gadgets）。以 H₂ 分子 Hamiltonian 模拟为概念验证，将问题块编码为 QSVT 电路，利用 GRAPE 算法生成复合脉冲，并在 3 个计算离子（2 系统 + 1 辅助）上通过含噪声 Lindblad 主方程仿真进行评估。

Result: 初步观察表明，该策略实现了显著的时序压缩，减少了总脉冲调度时长；同时，整体化合成操作消除了与离散脉冲查找开销相关的控制层延迟。

Conclusion: 通过简化物理控制调度，该方法为在更短时间内执行操作提供了可行路径，凸显了复合脉冲小工具在 T₂ 退相干基本极限内增加可达到计算深度的潜力。

Abstract: Standard gate-level transpilation introduces significant physical noise and overhead for high-precision quantum algorithms, such as the Quantum Singular Value Transformation (QSVT), on near-term trapped-ion hardware. Current compilers treat quantum operations as discrete units, forcing the physical control layer to execute highly fragmented laser pulses. To address this hardware-software disconnect, this work introduces a holistic pulse synthesis strategy that bypasses discrete gate-stitching to compile algorithms directly into continuous compound pulse gadgets. As a proof-of-concept, we target Hamiltonian simulation of the $H_2$ molecule, block-encoding the problem into a QSVT circuit to approximate the time-evolution operator $U = e^{-i H t}$ across 3 computational ions (2 system, 1 ancilla). We utilize the Gradient Ascent Pulse Engineering (GRAPE) algorithm to generate these compound gadgets and evaluate our methodology using noisy Lindblad master equation simulations. Preliminary observations indicate that the proposed strategy achieves significant temporal compression, reducing the total pulse schedule duration compared to standard compilers. Furthermore, synthesizing operations holistically eliminates the control-layer latency associated with discrete pulse lookup overhead. By streamlining the physical control schedule, this methodology offers a promising pathway to execute operations faster, highlighting the potential for compound gadgets to increase the computational depth achievable within fundamental $T_2$ decoherence limits.

</details>


### [129] [Simulating generic single-qubit open-dynamics via polarization-frequency coupling in a photonic interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00835)
*Kalle Raikisto,Alberto Ferrara,Tom Kuusela,Rosario Lo Franco,Jyrki Piilo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于光子偏振-频率耦合的开放量子系统模拟平台，通过马赫-曾德尔干涉仪和双折射石英板实现任意单比特开放系统动力学的高精度模拟，包括退极化与非马尔可夫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有石英板仅能模拟纯退相位过程，无法实现布居数与相干性之间的可控耦合。作者希望构建一种可调谐的光学协议，模拟更丰富的开放系统动力学（如退极化和非马尔可夫演化）。

Method: 利用单个光子在开放马赫-曾德尔干涉仪中传播，通过双折射石英板耦合偏振（系统）与频率（环境）自由度，将频率视为有效环境，解析推导约化偏振动力学，通过调节光子频率分布和干涉仪参数实现目标单比特态的可控生成。

Result: 该方案能够高效再现目标单比特态，在退极化和非马尔可夫动力学等基准测试中实现了高精度。

Conclusion: 偏振-频率工程化的光子干涉仪是一种通用的开放量子系统模拟协议，在可实现的实验条件下具有高灵活性和准确性。

Abstract: We propose a photonic platform for simulating arbitrary single-qubit open-system dynamics using a single photon in an open Mach-Zehnder interferometer. A birefringent quartz plate induces a coupling between the polarization and frequency degrees of freedom. By treating the latter as an effective environment, we analytically derive the reduced polarization dynamics. We show that the resulting evolution is characterized by a controllable interplay between populations and coherence, instead of the usual dephasing caused by quartz plates. By adjusting the photon frequency distribution and interferometric parameters, we demonstrate that target single-qubit states can be efficiently reproduced through a tunable optical protocol expected to work under accessible experimental conditions. The simulator is benchmarked against paradigmatic open-system evolutions, including depolarization and non-Markovian dynamics, achieving high accuracy. Our results establish polarization-frequency engineered photonic interferometers as a versatile protocol for simulation of open quantum systems.

</details>


### [130] [Twisted Gaussian Schell States in Quantum Optics: Twist-Assisted Nonclassicality and Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00837)
*Fabricio Toscano,G. Cañas,A. Z. Khoury,P. H. Souto Ribeiro,S. P. Walborn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入Twisted Gaussian Schell (TGS)态——经典扭曲高斯谢尔模型光束的量子光学类比，证明其非经典性源于扭曲参数导致的全局正交分量压缩，并在四模扩展中实现可调控纠缠激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立经典光束工程与量子信息之间的双向桥梁，探索扭曲相位在量子光学中的非经典效应及其纠缠生成能力。

Method: 定义TGS态为两模混合高斯态，通过非对称两模热态经移相器、分束器后施加局域压缩实现扭曲参数。分析全局正交分量压缩、联合光子数分布解析形式，以及通过平衡分束器耦合辅助真空态构建四模纠缠结构。

Result: TGS态在扭曲参数非零时存在全局正交分量压缩（低于散粒噪声极限），联合光子数分布呈现非经典特征；四模扩展中2×2二分块出现纠缠，1×3二分块无纠缠；固定输入压缩下增大扭曲参数可激活并加深纠缠。

Conclusion: 经典物理性边界与量子物理性条件一致，扭曲参数是调控非经典性和纠缠的关键资源，推进了经典光束整形与量子信息处理的理论融合。

Abstract: We introduce the Twisted Gaussian Schell (TGS) state, a two-mode mixed Gaussian state defined as the quantum-optical analog of the Twisted Gaussian Schell-model beam of classical paraxial optics, characterized by the so-called twist phase. In the TGS state, the twist parameter arises when an asymmetric two-mode thermal state is subject to local squeezing after the action of phase shifters and a beam splitter. Its defining quantum feature is nonclassicality: although the state is separable in its natural bipartition, when the twist parameter is nonzero there are global quadratures that can be squeezed below the shot-noise limit. The nonclassicality has also a direct signature in the joint photon-number distribution, which we obtain in closed form. Moreover, coupling each mode to an ancillary vacuum at a balanced beam splitter yields a four-mode state with entanglement in select $2\times2$ bipartitions, with local description given by two TGS states, and all $1\times3$ bipartitions.
  For fixed input squeezing, increasing the twist parameter activates entanglement where the state is otherwise separable and deepens it where already present. The classical physicality bound on the twist parameter coincides with the quantum physicality condition. These results advance the two-way bridge between classical beam engineering and quantum information.

</details>


### [131] [The Dynamical Lie Algebra of QAOA-MaxCut on the Complete Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00945)
*Jonathan Allcock,Pei Yuan,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了完整图上QAOA-MaxCut问题的动力学李代数解析表达式，证明了损失函数方差随量子比特数线性增长，解决了[ASYZ26]中的开放问题，确认了此类系统不出现贫瘠高原现象。证明基于将动力学李代数生成元投影到由酉群与对称群不可约表示间的Schur-Weyl对偶性给出的子空间上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决[ASYZ26]中提出的开放问题，即完整图上的QAOA-MaxCut问题是否存在贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）。

Method: 利用Schur-Weyl对偶性，将动力学李代数生成元投影到酉群与对称群不可约表示对应的子空间上，从而解析地推导出动力学李代数的结构。

Result: 完整图上QAOA-MaxCut的动力学李代数具有解析表达式，且损失函数方差与量子比特数成线性关系，即不出现贫瘠高原。

Conclusion: 完整图上的QAOA-MaxCut系统在训练中不会遭遇梯度消失问题（贫瘠高原），这为QAOA在该图结构上的应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We give an analytical expression for the dynamical Lie algebra corresponding to the QAOA-MaxCut problem on complete graphs, and show that the variance of the associated loss function scales linearly in the number of qubits. This solves an open problem from [ASYZ26] and confirms that such systems do not exhibit barren plateaus. The proof is based on projecting the dynamical Lie algebra generators onto subspaces given by the Schur-Weyl duality between irreducible representations of the unitary and symmetric groups.

</details>


### [132] [Bridging Quantum Computing Paradigms toward Semiconductor Yield: A Controlled CV-versus-DV Comparison on Wafer-Map Defect Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00961)
*Yeonhong Kim,Jonghyeok Im,Monu Nath Baitha,Kyoungsik Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在WM-811K晶圆图谱缺陷分类任务上，系统比较了连续变量（CV）和离散变量（DV）量子神经网络（QNN）的性能。在共享卷积骨干网络（约430万参数）下，仅替换头部为经典全连接、CV-QNN或DV-QNN，CV头部（4 qumodes时79.7%准确率）显著优于DV头部（4 qubits时61.6%），优势源于结构化连续相空间编码而非希尔伯特空间维度。两个量子头部均低于经典基线（85.0%），但CV在空间局部缺陷（Edge-Loc）上表现突出（召回率0.66 vs DV≤0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界实现量子神经网络需要明确哪种量子计算范式（CV或DV）更适合特定任务。受AI加速器和高带宽存储器中晶圆级缺陷筛选重要性的启发，本研究旨在可控条件下系统比较两种范式的实际性能差异。

Method: 使用WM-811K晶圆图谱数据集（8类缺陷），设计共享卷积骨干网络（约430万参数），仅替换头部为三种结构：经典全连接层、CV-QNN（3/4/8 qumodes）、DV-QNN（3/4/8 qubits）。CV使用Fock截断d=2，DV使用标准量子门。在相同条件下训练和评估，并在IBM量子硬件上测试DV性能。

Result: CV头部以4 qumodes达到79.7±1.8%准确率，DV头部以4 qubits仅61.6±1.4%，差距达18个百分点。在Edge-Loc类上CV召回率0.66±0.06，DV≤0.05。训练曲线显示DV存在表征容量上限而非优化失败。在IBM硬件上，DV在浅层电路保持性能，深度电路才退化。经典基线为85.0%。

Conclusion: CV-QNN在结构化空间特征提取上优于DV-QNN，优势源于连续相空间编码和类似神经网络的层次结构，而非简单的高维希尔伯特空间优势。尽管两种量子头部均未超越经典基线，但控制实验表明CV范式在结构化感知任务上更具潜力，为未来量子优势的范式和任务匹配提供了指导。

Abstract: Realizing quantum neural networks (QNNs) in industry requires knowing which quantum computing paradigm suits which task. Motivated by AI accelerators and high-bandwidth memory, where die stacking makes wafer-level defect screening central to yield, we study WM-811K wafer-map defect classification (eight classes), comparing the dominant paradigms, continuous-variable (CV) and discrete-variable (DV), under controlled conditions. To isolate the quantum circuit as the sole variable, a shared convolutional backbone (~4.3M parameters) feeds interchangeable heads (classical dense, CV-QNN, or DV-QNN) as the only structural difference; each quantum head is scaled over three sizes (3, 4, 8 qumodes/qubits). The CV head consistently outperforms the DV head: at four qumodes/qubits it reaches 79.7 +/- 1.8% accuracy versus 61.6 +/- 1.4%, a non-overlapping 18-point gap. The advantage is sharpest on the spatially localized Edge-Loc class, easily confused with Scratch, which CV recovers with recall 0.66 +/- 0.06 while DV fails at every size (<=0.05), showing the structured CV layer better captures fine spatial distinctions between defect types. Training curves show the DV limitation is a representational-capacity ceiling, not an optimization failure; at the Fock cutoff used here (d = 2) the CV advantage reflects two intrinsic properties, a structured, neural-network-analogue layer and continuous phase-space encoding, not Hilbert-space dimensionality. On IBM hardware, DV accuracy holds at shallow depth, degrading only at the deepest circuit. Both quantum heads remain below the classical baseline (85.0%), but the controlled setting isolates where a structured head already helps and, as noise and scale improve, which paradigm can deliver practical advantage.

</details>


### [133] [Limitations of Error Model Approximations in Quantum Network Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00998)
*Julia Freund,Jorge Miguel-Ramiro,Julius Wallnöfer,Wolfgang Dür*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，在量子网络的大规模经典模拟中，若使用简化噪声模型（如Pauli twirling或reset信道），会严重低估或高估协议性能，且无法捕捉到保真度振荡等关键现象，强调必须对完整噪声架构进行严格验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络的大规模经典模拟常依赖噪声近似（如Pauli twirling、reset信道等），但这些简化模型是否可靠、会对协议性能预测造成多大偏差，尚未被系统评估。

Method: 研究通过对比简化噪声模型与完整噪声模型在迭代性和顺序性协议（如纠缠纯化、纠缠交换、中继链）中的表现，分析了微小误差如何累积并导致性能预测的定量和定性偏差。

Result: 简化噪声模型会导致性能的严重高估或低估、产生测量结果依赖性，并完全遗漏真实噪声下出现的保真度振荡等关键行为。

Conclusion: 对未来量子技术中操作阈值的准确预测而言，必须对完整噪声架构进行严格的验证，而不能仅依赖简化噪声近似。

Abstract: Efficient classical simulation of large-scale quantum networks frequently relies on noise approximations, which consider a restricted set of operators to describe noisy channels and operations. In this work, we demonstrate how such simplified error models, such as Pauli twirling or reset channels, can lead to severe quantitative and qualitative discrepancies in protocol performance predictions. We analyze, in particular, how small differences can accumulate in iterative and sequential protocols such as entanglement purification, entanglement swapping, and repeater chains. Our results reveal that neglected error contributions can lead to important performance under- and over-estimations, measurement-outcome dependency, and oscillations in the fidelity, which are entirely overlooked by the simplified error model approximations. These results show that rigorous validation of complete noise architectures is indispensable for accurately predicting operational thresholds in future quantum technologies.

</details>


### [134] [Optimizing Symmetry Informed Probabilistic Error Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01072)
*Tom O'Leary,Daniel J. Egger,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将量子错误检测（QED）与概率误差消除（PEC）相结合，通过优化对称性测量配置，相比单独使用PEC能获得更准确、方差更低的估计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子错误检测（QED）与概率误差消除（PEC）两种量子误差缓解（QEM）技术的协同效应，解决单独使用PEC时估计精度和方差不够理想的问题。

Method: 将噪声对称性测量的配置选择建模为经典优化问题，系统抑制噪声影响；在GHZ态输出分布和广义超快编码Fermi-Hubbard模型时间动力学模拟中应用优化的PEC+QED方法。

Result: PEC+QED在GHZ态和Fermi-Hubbard模型上均优于单独PEC；GHZ态需优化对称性测量配置才能获得优势；对于更大规模的Fermi-Hubbard系统，可通过仅测量部分稳定子来降低缓解开销。

Conclusion: 电路特定的QEM技术定制至关重要，容错设计原理对近期的量子设备已具有实际价值。

Abstract: We show that combining quantum error detection (QED) with probabilistic error cancellation (PEC) gives more accurate and lower-variance estimates than PEC alone, provided that the symmetry measurements required for QED are carefully chosen. Because noisy symmetry measurements can negate the benefits of the PEC+QED approach, we cast the selection of measurement configurations as a classical optimization problem that systematically suppresses the impact of noise. Applying optimized PEC+QED to GHZ-state output distributions and to simulating the time-dynamics of a generalized superfast encoded Fermi-Hubbard model, we find consistent improvements over PEC. For GHZ states, the optimization over symmetry measurement configurations is essential for achieving an advantage. For the Fermi-Hubbard model, PEC+QED improves observable estimation on a $2 \times 2$ lattice and for larger systems the mitigation overheads can be reduced by measuring only subsets of stabilizers. Our results demonstrate the importance of circuit-specific tailoring of QEM techniques and that fault-tolerant design principles may already provide value for near-term devices.

</details>


### [135] [Fisher Glasses: Tail-Certified Quantum Metrology in Quenched Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01085)
*El Mustapha Mansouri,Keigo Arai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"淬火尾认证信息"（quenched tail-certified information）概念，证明平均Fisher信息（包括QFI）无法认证淬火传感器中的量子计量优势，必须考虑会话分辨的Fisher几何。通过逆损失尾指数β≤1的非可积性相变，揭示了Fisher零点的罕见事件统计主导的"Fisher玻璃"相，并在浅层NV色心Ramsey实验中验证了平均QFI优化会导致尾部灾难，而尾认证设计可恢复近三个数量级的认证信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子计量优势认证依赖于平均化Fisher响应（对比度、磁化率、QFI），但这在淬火传感器中失效——慢环境变量在单次会话内冻结、跨会话变化（如浅层NV色心、超导量子比特中的慢速两级涨落器、半导体自旋量子比特中的电荷噪声漂移）。需要建立一套基于会话分辨Fisher几何而非平均信道的认证框架。

Method: 提出潜在会话条件认证方法：对潜在会话变量做条件化、投影干扰方向、反演得到可达损失，然后对尾部进行认证。定义逆上尾损失为"淬火尾认证信息"。证明无go定理：任何平均Fisher数据都无法确定该认证量。通过Fisher零点可积性相变（尾指数β）划分边界：β≤1时认证损失不可积，即使退火信息很大。

Result: 认证的量子资源是横向于潜在无序的响应，而非共享其生成器的原始放大。给出通用设计法则：安全窗口、非简并组合、Fisher储备、作用量分离、Fisher截断准则。在浅层NV Ramsey实验中，平均QFI优化导致尾部灾难，而尾认证设计在相同射击预算和潜在系综下恢复近三个数量级的认证信息。

Conclusion: 揭示非自平均的"Fisher玻璃"相，其由Fisher零点的罕见事件统计支配。传统基于平均Fisher信息的计量优化在淬火噪声环境下具有根本性缺陷，必须转向尾部认证框架。

Abstract: Quantum metrological advantage is certified by averaged Fisher responses: contrast, susceptibility, or quantum Fisher information (QFI). This fails in quenched sensors, where slow environmental variables freeze within a session but vary between repetitions: shallow nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers, superconducting qubits with slow two-level fluctuators, and semiconductor spin qubits in drifting charge noise. They sample session-resolved Fisher geometries, not an averaged channel. Certification conditions on the latent session, projects nuisance directions, inverts to attainable loss, then tail-certifies; this inverse upper-tail loss defines quenched tail-certified information. A no-go theorem: no averaged Fisher data determine this certificate; ensembles sharing averaged Fisher matrix, QFI, and projected information have finite or zero certified precision. A Fisher-zero integrability transition governs collapse: the inverse-loss tail exponent $β$ sets the boundary, with nonintegrable certified loss for $β\le 1$, even when annealed information is large or scaling. The certified quantum resource is response transverse to latent disorder, not raw amplification sharing its generator; universal design laws: safe windows, nondegenerate portfolios, Fisher reserves, action separation, Fisher-cut criteria. A shallow-NV Ramsey tournament shows average-QFI optimization is tail-catastrophic, whereas tail-certified designs recover nearly three orders of magnitude in certified information at equal shot budget and latent ensemble. These non-self-averaging phases are Fisher glasses, governed by Fisher-zero rare-event statistics.

</details>


### [136] [Entanglement-spectrum fingerprint of a non-invertible symmetry: the Kramers--Wannier duality defect on the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01137)
*Yi Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了临界Ising链中Kramers-Wannier对偶缺陷（量子维度d_σ=√2）的范畴数据编码在基态单粒子纠缠谱中：一个最大混合的马约拉纳零模是边界熵log g=(1/2)log 2的谱起源，进而决定了d_σ本身。通过转移矩阵动量偏移和Casimir曲率两个独立路径确认了扭曲场权重h_σ=1/16，缺陷希尔伯特空间组织为半整数σ-扭曲共形塔，将边界熵从积分数提升为不可逆性的能级分辨谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非可逆对称性以无理量子维度的拓扑缺陷为特征，但其对量子多体态纠缠谱的影响尚未在能谱层面得到解决。本文旨在揭示这种印记的微观谱结构。

Method: 以临界Ising链的Kramers-Wannier对偶缺陷为规范案例，分析其基态的单粒子纠缠谱、转移矩阵动量偏移和Casimir曲率，重建缺陷希尔伯特空间的共形塔结构。

Result: 发现最大混合马约拉纳零模是边界熵log g=(1/2)log 2的谱起源；通过两个独立路径确认扭曲场权重h_σ=1/16；缺陷希尔伯特空间形成半整数σ-扭曲共形塔。

Conclusion: 将边界熵从整体积分量提升为可分辨能级的谱特征，为非可逆性的诊断提供了精确可解的标定靶，可用于张量网络研究中缺乏自由费米子捷径的对偶缺陷。

Abstract: Non-invertible symmetries are characterized by topological defects of irrational quantum dimension, but their imprint on the entanglement of a quantum many-body state has not been resolved at the level of the spectrum. We show that the categorical data of the canonical example -- the Kramers--Wannier (KW) duality defect of the critical Ising chain, with quantum dimension d_sigma=sqrt(2) -- is encoded in the single-particle entanglement spectrum of its ground state: a maximally mixed Majorana zero mode is the spectral origin of the boundary entropy log g=(1/2)log 2, hence of d_sigma itself. Reading the same duality-twisted ground state along two independent routes -- the transfer-matrix momentum shift and the Casimir curvature of the energy -- pins the twist-field weight h_sigma=1/16 twice over, and the defect Hilbert space organizes into a half-integer sigma-twisted conformal tower. This promotes the boundary entropy from an integrated number to a level-resolved spectral signature of non-invertibility, and supplies an exactly solvable calibration target for tensor-network studies of duality defects that lack a free-fermion shortcut.

</details>


### [137] [Entanglement fingerprint of a non-invertible symmetry: exact Fibonacci cut charges on the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01151)
*Yi Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明临界黄金链中Fibonacci对偶缺陷在有限晶格尺寸下就具有精确的分类指纹，无需有限尺寸外推即可得到精确边界熵log g=log φ，并区分了这一精确两荷结果与更精细的六主三临界伊辛分辨率的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常不可逆缺陷需要通过缩放谱或红外CFT数据来诊断，本文旨在证明在有限晶格尺寸下就能直接获得非阿贝尔对偶缺陷的精确分类指纹，避免有限尺寸外推的依赖。

Method: 通过有限维算子恒等式分析夹层切割投影仪，结合偶长基态的Perron-Frobenius扇区定理，建立了精确的切割荷权重关系P_tau/P_1=φ^2。

Result: 得到了精确的边界熵log g=log φ，无需有限尺寸外推；偶长反铁磁基态具有固定的切割荷权重；区分了精确两荷结果与六主三临界伊辛分辨率的尺度极限。

Conclusion: 该工作为不可逆缺陷的晶格级诊断提供了新方法，展示了非阿贝尔对偶缺陷的精确边界熵可以直接从有限尺寸系统中获得。

Abstract: Non-invertible defects are usually diagnosed through scaling spectra or infrared CFT data. We show that the Fibonacci duality defect of the critical golden chain already carries an exact categorical fingerprint at finite lattice size. The even-length antiferromagnetic ground state has fixed cut-charge weights, giving P_tau/P_1=phi^2 and log g=log phi without finite-size extrapolation. The proof is a finite-dimensional operator identity for the sandwiched cut projectors, combined with a Perron-Frobenius sector theorem for the even-length ground state. This gives a sharp lattice-level boundary entropy for a non-Abelian duality defect. We also separate this exact two-charge result from the finer six-primary tricritical-Ising resolution: the latter is located by the standard scaling-limit Virasoro branching of A_4 affine-TL packets, and is not an assumption in the finite-size theorem.

</details>


### [138] [Non-Clifford Benchmarking via Ensemble Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01180)
*Stancho G. Stanchev,Nikolay V. Vitanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成特征选择（EFS）方法，通过离线训练和岭回归学习权重，从候选池中选取少量可执行的电路测量，快速估计多量子比特非克利福德门的工艺不保真度，在ibm_kingston上对CCZ门实现了约0.01的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非克利福德目标门（如CCZ），标准克利福德基准测试无法适用，需要一种快速且准确的工艺不保真度估计方法。

Method: 从候选测量池中通过离线训练（基于物理动机的噪声信道集成）选择紧凑的电路测量集，利用岭回归学习线性估计器权重，训练集可根据先验噪声知识进行调节。

Result: 在ibm_kingston上使用两种与CCZ电路结构相关的克利福德验证基准进行验证，EFS与独立交错随机基准测试（IRB）在0.02-0.2的不保真度范围内高度一致，估计精度约0.01。

Conclusion: EFS方法能够有效且准确地估计非克利福德多量子比特门的工艺不保真度，且可应用于实际量子设备。

Abstract: We propose an Ensemble Feature Selection (EFS) method for fast estimation of process infidelity of involutory multi-qubit gates, including non-Clifford targets, for which standard Clifford-based benchmarking does not apply. The method selects a compact set of experimentally executable circuit measurements from a candidate pool through offline training on a physically motivated ensemble of noisy channels, and combines them into a linear estimator with weights learned by ridge regression. The training ensemble is an explicit and tunable component of the protocol, incorporating prior knowledge about dominant hardware noise mechanisms. The estimator is validated on ibm_kingston using two Clifford validation benchmarks structurally related to the transpiled CCZ circuit, against independent Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking (IRB). Both show close EFS-IRB agreement across a wide range of process infidelities, with an estimation precision of approximately 0.01 over a process infidelity range of 0.02-0.2. EFS is subsequently applied directly to CCZ on the same device.

</details>


### [139] [Exploiting Symmetry in Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01187)
*Markus Baumann,Michael Poppel,Thomas Gabor,Maximilian Zorn,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien,Jonas Stein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子储备池计算（QRC）中，仅对称哈密顿量不足以保证对称性，必须通过可观测轨道完备化方法，在编码、动力学、测量和读出四个接口上对齐对称性，才能有效提升循环预测任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性是一种强大的归纳偏置，但在QRC中不能仅通过使储备池对称来施加，因为相关对称性必须在测量的特征图中可见。研究旨在解决循环预测任务中如何正确引入对称性的问题。

Method: 提出可观测轨道完备化方法，通过测量对称性相关的可观测通道，并对齐编码、动力学、测量和读出四个接口的对称性。

Result: 对称哈密顿量不足以保证对称性；即使大量泡利测量集也可能因通道不匹配数据对称性而失败；通过可观测轨道完备化，对齐所有四个接口可获得最大性能提升，并在自旋环、真实天气和IBM硬件实验中验证了测量跨度机制。

Conclusion: QRC中实现对称性需要编码、动力学、测量和读出四个接口的系统性对齐，仅对称哈密顿量或大量测量不足以保证对称归纳偏置的有效性。

Abstract: Symmetry is a powerful inductive bias, but in quantum reservoir computing (QRC) it cannot be imposed only by making the reservoir symmetric. QRC maps inputs through fixed quantum dynamics into nonlinear expectation-value features and trains only a classical readout, so the relevant symmetry must be visible in the measured feature map. We study cyclic forecasting tasks, such as sensors around a turbine or weather stations along a latitude circle, where the same local pattern should be forecast by the same rule wherever it appears on the ring. Thus, rotating the input by one site should rotate, not change, the predicted field. We show that a symmetric Hamiltonian is not enough: even large Pauli measurement sets can fail if their channels do not match the data symmetry, since optimization cannot recover channels that were never measured. We address this through observable-orbit completion, which measures symmetry-related observable channels and aligns encoding, dynamics, measurement, and readout. The strongest gains arise from aligning all four interfaces together, with matched spin-ring, real-weather, and IBM hardware checks supporting the same measured-span mechanism.

</details>


### [140] [Non-signaling assistance in prepare-and-measure scenarios with classical communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01193)
*José Nogueira,Carlos Vieira,Lucas E. A. Porto,Lucas Pollyceno,Rafael Rabelo,Otfried Gühne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了制备-测量（PM）场景中自适应与非自适应非信号（NS）辅助的经典通信能力。结果表明：非自适应NS辅助足以模拟相同消息维度的量子通信（如用经典dit加NS关联模拟qudit传输）；任何自适应NS优势都可归因于接收者无测量选择的场景，排除了纠缠辅助量子协议中真正多设置优势的可能性；并完整刻画了自适应NS策略严格优于非自适应策略的所有PM场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，在纠缠辅助的经典通信场景中，自适应策略（接收者利用传输的消息引导测量选择）可超越标准非自适应协议。然而，超出量子理论范畴时，这种优势的终极界限尚不清楚。本文旨在系统探索经典通信PM场景中自适应与非自适应非信号辅助的极限能力。

Method: 1) 对任意PM场景中非自适应和自适应NS辅助可实现的行为集进行简单刻画；2) 通过理论证明比较两种辅助方式的能力，包括量子通信的经典模拟；3) 分析自适应NS优势的本质结构，证明其可归约到接收者无测量选择的场景；4) 完整分类自适应NS策略具有严格优势的PM场景。

Result: 1) 非自适应NS辅助足以用经典dit模拟qudit的量子通信；2) 自适应NS优势本质上源于接收者无测量选择的场景，而非纠缠辅助量子协议中多测量设置的真正优势；3) 完整识别了所有自适应NS策略严格优于非自适应策略的PM场景。

Conclusion: 非自适应非信号辅助在PM场景中已具有强大能力，而自适应NS优势的结构比量子情形更为局限，这为理解非信号关联在经典通信中的根本作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: Extracting the full power of non-local correlations in prepare-and-measure (PM) scenarios requires precise control over the timing and structure of the receiver's measurements. Indeed, recent developments in entanglement-assisted classical communication scenarios have shown that adaptive strategies-where the receiver uses the transmitted message to guide their measurement choice-can outperform standard non-adaptive protocols. Moving beyond quantum theory, however, the ultimate limits of such advantages remain largely unexplored. In this work, we thoroughly study adaptive and non-adaptive non-signaling (NS) assistance in PM scenarios with classical communication. We provide simple characterizations of the sets of behaviors that can be realized using both non-adaptive and adaptive NS assistance in arbitrary PM scenarios. As a consequence, we show that non-adaptive NS assistance is already strong enough to reproduce quantum communication with the same message dimension: the transmission of a qudit can be simulated by a classical dit assisted non-adaptively by NS correlations. We then compare adaptive and non-adaptive NS assistance. We prove that any adaptive NS advantage can be traced back to scenarios in which the receiver has no measurement choice, ruling out the genuinely multi-setting advantages found in entanglement-assisted quantum protocols. Finally, we identify all PM scenarios where adaptive NS strategies provide a strict advantage over non-adaptive ones.

</details>


### [141] [Diverse efficiency of observable optimization for four-level quantum systems with higher-order traps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01217)
*Alexander Pechen,Boris Volkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对特殊四能级量子系统（V-V系统和anharmonic系统）的量子控制景观进行了解析和数值分析，证明了零控制是一个五阶陷阱点。通过GRAPE和GPM算法实验，发现V-V系统的优化效率在远离零控制时急剧提升至100%，而链式相互作用系统则提升缓慢甚至下降，这种差异可能与目标泛函二阶导数为零的控制子空间精细结构有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示特殊四能级量子系统（V-V系统和anharmonic系统）中量子控制景观的陷阱结构与优化效率之间的关系，特别是理解不同系统哈密顿量下优化效率差异的根源。

Method: 采用解析与数值相结合的方法：理论上证明零控制为五阶陷阱点，数值上使用GRAPE算法（无约束）和GPM（有约束）研究优化效率行为。

Result: V-V系统的优化效率在远离零控制一定距离时急剧上升至100%，而具有链式相互作用的系统效率提升更慢且幅度更小，甚至出现小幅下降。

Conclusion: 不同系统哈密顿量下优化效率的显著差异可能与目标泛函二阶导数为零的控制子空间的精细结构有关。

Abstract: In this work, we perform an analytical and numerical analysis of quantum landscapes for controlling special four-level quantum systems for which we prove that the null control is a five-order trap: a $V-V$ system and an anharmonic system. As a control goal, an observable optimization is considered. The rigorous theoretical analysis is followed by the numerical experiments based on the GRadient Ascent Pulse Engineering (GRAPE) algorithm and Gradient Projection Method (GPM), performed to investigate the behavior of the efficiency of optimization for unconstrained (using GRAPE) and constrained (using GPM) controls. As the main result, we observe an interesting phenomenon with a diverse behavior of the optimization efficiency depending on the system Hamiltonian -- sharp increase of the optimization efficiency up to 100% at certain distance from the null control for a V-V system, while much slower and less significant increase (and even small decrease) for a system with the chain interaction. This sharp difference might be related with the fine structure of the subspace of controls where second derivative of the objective functional is zero.

</details>

### [142] [Gravitating Tubes Beyond World Line Paradigm In General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00036)
*Abhi Savaliya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出用余维数为零的"引力管"替代点粒子来描述引力物质，以克服Geroch-Traschen障碍（高余维源在广义相对论中不存在光滑解）。管状结构内的集体应力-能量张量是光滑的，在紫外极限下可还原为点粒子作用量，且静止质量作为有效量而非基本参数出现。扰动静力学分析表明标量场是类cuscuton的非动力学场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典点粒子描述的应力-能量张量是分布性的（集中在世界线上），对于余维数≥2的源，Geroch-Traschen定理证明了光滑时空解普遍不存在。这一障碍促使人们寻找更高维的、余维数为零的物质描述，以保持与广义相对论的数学兼容性。

Method: 在辅助类时曲线的管状邻域内构造类时管，管内部由类时余维一超曲面层析，其动力学由膜作用量控制。通过适当的拉格朗日密度标度，在紫外极限（管半径→0）下将管作用量约化为点粒子作用量及规范的自力项。扰动静力学分析包括场扰动（标量场为cuscuton-like非动力学场）和叶面微小形变（导出Jacobi方程）。

Result: 集体应力-能量张量光滑，无点粒子奇异性；对一大类张力和势能轮廓，管内强能量条件被违反，而弱能量条件和零能量条件仍满足；紫外极限下恢复点粒子描述，静止质量为有效量；无限声速表明层析生成标量场是非动力学的。

Conclusion: 引力管在几何上和动力学上是一种自洽的物质描述，尊重Geroch-Traschen障碍，为广义相对论中物质的引力描述提供了新的框架。

Abstract: The simplest point-particle description of classical matter is incompatible with Einstein's General Relativity because the stress-energy tensor of a point particle is distributional and concentrated on a one-dimensional worldline. For such higher-codimension sources, smooth spacetime solutions generally do not exist. This obstruction was established by Geroch and Traschen for sources of codimension $\geq2$.
  Motivated by this result, this thesis proposes codimension-zero tubes as a fundamental description of gravitating matter. Timelike tubes are constructed within the tubular neighbourhood of an auxiliary timelike curve. The tube interior is foliated by timelike codimension-one hypersurfaces whose dynamics are governed by a brane-like action. The resulting collective stress-energy tensor is smooth, unlike that of a point particle. For a broad class of tension and potential profiles, the strong energy condition is violated inside the tube, while the null and weak energy conditions remain satisfied. In the ultraviolet limit, where the tube radius vanishes, an appropriate rescaling of the Lagrangian density reduces the tube action to the point-particle action together with a canonical self-force-like term. The particle's rest mass then emerges as an effective quantity rather than a fundamental localized parameter.
  Perturbative stability is analysed at two levels. Field perturbations yield an infinite squared sound speed, showing that the foliation-generating scalar is non-dynamical and cuscuton-like. Small deformations of the leaves lead to the Jacobi equation for timelike hypersurface congruences, further constraining admissible tension and potential profiles. These results establish gravitating tubes as a geometrically and dynamically consistent description of matter that respects the Geroch--Traschen obstruction.

</details>


### [143] [Acoustic Hawking radiation as a tunnelling effect in Michel accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00037)
*Nisha Jangid,Arnab K. Ray*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了米歇尔吸积过程中的声学黑洞特性，发现稳态入流在鞍点处变为跨声速，欧拉扰动产生声学黑洞度量，高频行波不会破坏稳态，声波在声障处被阻挡但可通过隧穿效应传播，霍金温度和霍金声子频率因时空几何而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索米歇尔吸积流在鞍点处成为跨声速的动力学特性，以及该过程中产生的声学黑洞效应，特别是扰动稳定性、声波隧穿和霍金辐射的增强机制。

Method: 采用欧拉扰动方法分析稳态入流，构建声学黑洞度量，通过高频行波分析研究扰动稳定性，利用量子隧穿理论分析声波通过声障的行为，计算霍金温度及霍金声子频率的时空几何增强效应。

Result: 稳态入流在鞍点处变为跨声速；欧拉扰动可产生声学黑洞度量；高频行波扰动不会导致稳态入流失稳；向外传播的声波在声障处被阻挡，但可通过隧穿效应以指数衰减振幅通过；霍金温度和霍金声子频率因时空几何而得到增强。

Conclusion: 米歇尔吸积流中的声学黑洞模型展现了丰富的物理现象，包括跨声速转变、扰动稳定性、声波隧穿以及增强的霍金辐射，为研究黑洞物理与流体力学的类比提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: Michel accretion becomes transonic at the saddle point of a dynamical system. An Eulerian perturbation on the steady inflow produces the metric of an acoustic black hole. As a high-frequency travelling wave the perturbation does not destabilize the steady inflow. Acoustic waves propagating outwards against the fluid inflow are blocked at the sonic barrier but can tunnel through it with an exponentially decaying amplitude. The Hawking temperature and the frequency of the Hawking phonons are enhanced by the spacetime geometry.

</details>


### [144] [Modified Cosmology from Mass-to-Horizon Relation: Background Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00133)
*Pranav Prasanthan,Hussain Gohar,Vincenzo Salzano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了质量-视界关系在宇宙学中的含义，发现该框架下的广义熵形式必须严格接近标准Bekenstein-Hawking面积律才能产生可行的宇宙学演化。幂律纠缠修正可在极窄参数空间内产生早期暗能量，而量子引力修正因Planck标度压制而不可观测。所有可行模型最终都预测出类似ΛCDM的当前宇宙学背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于质量-视界关系的热力学一致广义熵泛函在宇宙学中的可行性，检验其是否能够产生与观测相符的宇宙演化序列（辐射-物质-暗能量）。

Method: 采用Cai-Kim热力学第一定律形式，推导相应的修正Friedmann方程，分析背景宇宙学演化，并对不同熵形式（包括标准Bekenstein-Hawking熵、幂律纠缠修正、量子引力修正）的参数空间进行约束。

Result: (1) 仅当广义熵非常接近标准Bekenstein-Hawking面积律时，宇宙学演化才可行；(2) 幂律纠缠修正只能在极窄参数空间内产生中等程度的早期暗能量成分；(3) 量子引力修正被Planck标度压制，观测上无关紧要；(4) 所有可行模型在当前时代的宇宙学背景均表现为ΛCDM-like。

Conclusion: 背景宇宙学单独就对这类热力学一致的广义熵构造施加了严格限制，排除了显著偏离标准面积律的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological implications of the mass-to-horizon relation, which provides a unified framework for thermodynamically consistent generalized horizon-entropy functionals. Using the Cai-Kim formulation of the first law of thermodynamics, we derive the corresponding modified Friedmann equations and examine the resulting background evolution. We find that cosmological viability sharply restricts admissible deviations from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law: phenomenologically acceptable scenarios are confined to a narrow neighborhood of the standard entropy, while more pronounced deviations generically spoil the standard radiation-matter-dark-energy sequence. Power-law entanglement corrections can give rise to a moderate early-dark-energy component, but only within a tightly constrained region of parameter space, whereas quantum-gravity corrections are suppressed by the Planck scale and remain observationally irrelevant. Consequently, all viable models predict a $Λ$CDM-like cosmological background at the present epoch. These findings demonstrate that background cosmology alone imposes stringent constraints on thermodynamically consistent generalized entropy constructions of this class.

</details>


### [145] [Cosmology with a Non-minimally Coupled Dark Matter Fluid II. Cosmological Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00137)
*Samuele Silveravalle,Andrea Lapi,Francesco Benetti,Stefano Liberati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合暗物质与引力的宇宙学模型，分析了线性扰动演化，发现加速膨胀阶段产生蓝谱与观测不符，而反弹解能产生近标度不变的标量功率谱且张标比符合约束，为原初扰动产生提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质与引力非最小耦合的宇宙学后果，特别是能否替代暴胀或反弹模型来解释原初扰动的起源。

Method: 推导标量、矢量和张量扰动的演化方程，在相关宇宙学时期求解析解，分析功率谱特征并与观测比较。

Result: 加速膨胀阶段产生强蓝标量功率谱，与观测不相容；反弹解产生近标度不变标量功率谱，张标比符合当前约束，无需额外标量场。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合暗物质可能为原初宇宙学扰动的产生提供可行的替代机制，但需要更精细的处理来完善分析。

Abstract: We extend our study of a cosmological scenario in which dark matter is non-minimally coupled to gravity at the fluid level. In previous work, we showed that this interaction can drive an early phase of accelerated expansion, addressing the horizon and flatness problems, and can also lead to a cosmological bounce in the presence of spatial curvature. Here we analyse the evolution of linear perturbations in this framework. We derive the equations governing scalar, vector and tensor perturbations, and obtain analytic solutions in the relevant cosmological regimes. We find that perturbations generated during the accelerated expansion phase produce a strongly blue scalar power spectrum and are therefore incompatible with observations. By contrast, in bouncing solutions primordial fluctuations can originate during the contracting phase before the bounce. In this case, the model yields an approximately scale-invariant scalar power spectrum while keeping the tensor-to-scalar ratio compatible with current bounds, without introducing additional scalar fields. Although our treatment relies on simplifying approximations that should be refined in future work, these results indicate that non-minimally coupled dark matter may provide a viable alternative mechanism for the generation of primordial cosmological perturbations.

</details>


### [146] [Cosmological implications of f(T, B) gravity: constraints from recent observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00169)
*Laxmipriya Pati,Santosh V. Lohakare,S. K. Maurya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了修正的边界项teleparallel引力理论，通过幂律模型$f(T, B) = -T + \alpha(-B)^\beta$来探讨晚期宇宙加速膨胀和暗能量问题。利用宇宙计时器、Pantheon Plus和DESI BAO DR2数据集的MCMC分析约束参数，并与$\Lambda$CDM模型比较。AIC分析表明CC+PPS数据组合强烈支持$f(T,B)$模型，缓解了$H_0$张力，且模型展现出动态幻象分割线穿越行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决晚期宇宙加速膨胀和暗能量问题，通过引入边界项修正teleparallel引力理论，提供一种统一描述连接torsion和曲率基引力理论，并在适当极限下复现$f(T)$和$f(R)$引力。

Method: 采用幂律模型$f(T, B) = -T + \alpha(-B)^\beta$，通过MCMC分析使用宇宙计时器(CC)、Pantheon Plus样本(PPS)和DESI BAO DR2数据集约束模型参数和宇宙学参数，利用AIC进行模型比较。

Result: CC+PPS数据组合的AIC分析强烈支持$f(T,B)$模型优于$\Lambda$CDM，模型缓解了$H_0$张力（但需进一步统计显著性分析），并展现出动态幻象分割线穿越行为。

Conclusion: $f(T,B)$模型能有效描述晚期宇宙观测，提供比$\Lambda$CDM更好的现象学拟合，缓解哈勃常数张力，并给出可行的膨胀历史和暗能量演化特征。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the theoretical framework of modified teleparallel gravity with the inclusion of the boundary term in the action and investigate its cosmological implications by considering the power-law model $f(T, B) = -T + α(-B)^β$, with the aim of addressing the late-time accelerated expansion and the dark energy. In this context, $T$ denotes the torsion scalar and $B$ represents the boundary term, whose presence allows for departures from standard teleparallel dynamics and provides a unified description that connects torsion and curvature-based formulations, reproducing $f(T)$ and $f(R)$ gravity in appropriate limits. The viability of the model is assessed by confronting its theoretical predictions with observational data while constraining the cosmological and model parameters through a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis using cosmic chronometers (CC), the Pantheon Plus sample (PPS), and the DESI baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) Data Release 2 (DR2) datasets, and comparing its performance with the standard $Λ$CDM model. The Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) analysis shows that the combined CC+PPS dataset strongly favors the $f(T, B)$ model, suggesting an improved phenomenological fit to late-time observations relative to $Λ$CDM. Our result further shows an alleviation of H0 tensions, although a dedicated analysis is required to establish its full statistical significance. Furthermore, the background cosmological quantities indicate that the model exhibits a dynamical phantom-divide crossing while remaining consistent with late-time observations and yielding a viable expansion history and characteristic dark energy evolution.

</details>


### [147] [Bondi Accretion onto a Damour-Solodukhin Wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00388)
*R. M. Yusupova,R. Kh. Karimov,A. Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究发现Damour-Solodukhin虫洞作为Schwarzschild黑洞的模拟体时，其参数λ无需极端微小（λ~0），即使在λ~1时，虫洞的Bondi吸积轮廓在视界附近仍能有效模拟黑洞特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为DSWH若要模拟SBH，参数λ必须极端微小（λ~0），本文旨在检验这一要求的必要性，特别是从Bondi吸积轮廓的角度进行对比分析。

Method: 通过对比DSWH与SBH在Bondi吸积过程中的物质吸积轮廓，分析不同λ取值下两种天体的吸积特征相似性。

Result: 即使λ取值显著增大至λ~1，DSWH的某些吸积轮廓在视界附近仍能与SBH的对应特征高度吻合，表明极端微小λ并非必要条件。

Conclusion: DSWH作为SBH模拟体的参数约束可适度放宽，至少在Bondi吸积轮廓这一物理过程中，λ~1的虫洞仍能有效模拟黑洞视界附近的吸积行为。

Abstract: The Damour-Solodukhin wormhole (hereinafter DSWH) is known to mimic Schwarzschild black hole (hereinafter SBH) horizon in some properties. To act as a mimicker, the DSWH parameter $λ$ by definition is required to be extremely tiny, i.e., $λ\sim 0$. Our comparative analyses show that such a requirement may be too restrictive at least as far as the Bondi accretion profiles of the two objects, the DSWH and SBH, are concerned. Intriguingly, it turns out that some profiles of DSWH mimic those for the SBH near the horizon even at values of $λ$ considerably higher, i.e., $λ\sim 1$.

</details>


### [148] [Comments on the non-existence of unified hoop conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00390)
*A. Bhattacharya,R. N. Izmailov,R. Kh. Karimov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出Hod关于Thorne环猜想在准局部质量下不适用于Kerr-Newman黑洞的结论是正确的，但发现了一个有趣的例外：在弦理论的4维膜世界黑洞中，该猜想对静态和旋转潮汐带电黑洞均统一成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出Hod结论的局限性，并探索Thorne环猜想在更广义的膜世界黑洞场景下是否可能存在统一成立的特例。

Method: 理论分析，比较静态Reissner-Nordström黑洞与旋转Kerr-Newman黑洞在准局部质量定义下的环猜想成立情况，进而考察4维膜世界潮汐带电黑洞。

Result: 发现对于弦理论的4维膜世界潮汐带电黑洞，无论静态还是旋转情形，Thorne环猜想在相同的准局部质量定义下均统一成立。

Conclusion: Thorne环猜想在特定广义黑洞模型（膜世界潮汐带电黑洞）中可以实现静态与旋转情形的统一成立，这为理解该猜想的适用范围提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In a recent article, Hod [1] has concluded the non-existence, heretofore unnoticed, of a unified Thorne's hoop conjecture that holds for the quasi-local mass, $\mathcal{M}(r\leq R_{+})$ for static Reissner-Nordstrőm black hole, but does not hold for the spinning electrically charged Kerr-Newman black holes, when the same quasi-local mass is used. While the conclusion is correct and important, we wish to point out a curious exception for which the conjecture holds in a unified way for both the static and spinning \textit{tidal} charged $4d$ braneworld black holes of string theory, when the mass in the conjecture is the same quasi-local mass.

</details>


### [149] [Probing the chaos bound via spinning particles in Kerr-Newman-AdS spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00432)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang,Kangqiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Kerr-Newman-AdS时空中使用旋转试验粒子探究粒子与黑洞参数对混沌边界破坏的调控机制，发现混沌边界破坏由时空几何、电磁力和粒子动力学共同决定，粒子自旋通过轨道角动量耦合调节方向依赖性和参数阈值，负宇宙学常数作为势阱增强混沌行为，黑洞旋转与电荷存在竞争耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kerr-Newman-AdS时空中粒子自旋、黑洞参数（旋转、电荷、宇宙学常数）对混沌边界破坏的调控机制，揭示各参数之间的相互作用与竞争关系。

Method: 采用旋转试验粒子作为探针，在Kerr-Newman-AdS时空背景下系统分析粒子自旋、黑洞旋转、电荷及宇宙学常数对混沌边界破坏的影响。

Result: (1)粒子自旋通过轨道角动量耦合调节混沌破坏的方向依赖性和参数阈值；(2)负宇宙学常数作为势阱，其值越大混沌行为越强；(3)黑洞顺行旋转可抑制甚至完全消除电荷驱动的混沌破坏；(4)电荷是触发混沌破坏的条件，其效应受自旋稳定化调节；(5)Kerr-AdS极限下仅当黑洞反向旋转且旋转参数足够大、宇宙学常数足够小时发生破坏；(6)RN-AdS极限下电磁排斥比吸引更容易诱导混沌破坏。

Conclusion: Kerr-Newman-AdS时空中混沌边界破坏由时空几何、电磁力和粒子动力学协同调控，黑洞旋转与电荷存在竞争耦合，自旋起调节作用，不同参数极限下呈现不同的混沌破坏条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we employ spinning test particles as probes to investigate the regulatory effects of particle and black hole parameters on the violation of the chaos bound in Kerr-Newman-AdS spacetime. Our results demonstrate that the chaos bound violation is governed by the interplay of spacetime geometry, electromagnetic forces, and particle dynamics. The particle spin modulates the direction dependence and parameter thresholds of the violation through its coupling with the orbital angular momentum, which contributes to the total angular momentum. The negative cosmological constant acts as a potential well: a larger value enhances the chaotic behavior. A competitive coupling exists between the black hole rotation and charge -- its prograde rotation exerts a stabilizing effect that can suppress or even completely quench charge-driven violations, while the charge serves as a condition for triggering the violation, with its effect modulated by the spin stabilization. In the Kerr-AdS limit, the violation occurs only when the black hole rotates opposite to the $z$-axis with a sufficiently large rotation parameter and a sufficiently small cosmological constant. In the RN-AdS limit, the violation condition is jointly determined by the charge and the cosmological constant, with electromagnetic repulsion more readily inducing the violation than electromagnetic attraction.

</details>


### [150] [Reference Frames and Gravitational-Wave Polarizations: Symmetry Classification and Preferred-Frame Phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00539)
*Jie Zhu,Hao Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了引力波极化在洛伦兹boost下的变换规律，建立了基于对称性的新分类框架，发现了标量极化间的普适关系，以及在具有偏好框架的引力理论（如Bumblebee引力）中存在的极化混合效应和矢量-张量极化转换机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力波极化分类基于固定参考系（E(2)分类），未能考虑洛伦兹boost下极化模式的变化。作者希望建立一种观察者无关的分类框架，并探索偏好框架效应和洛伦兹破缺对引力波极化的影响。

Method: 推导了六种引力波极化在纵向和横向boost下的显式变换定律；对无偏好框架的引力理论，提出基于对称性的极化分类；对Bumblebee引力等有偏好框架的理论，分析双折射和极化混合效应。

Result: 发现传播模的五自由度中，纵向标量振幅与呼吸标量振幅满足普适关系 $A_l/A_b = -2(1-k^2/ω^2)$；在Bumblebee引力中，偏好框架导致显著的引力波双折射和依赖于观察者的极化混合；发现了矢量-张量极化转换机制——偏好框架中的矢量模对运动探测器必然产生可观测的张量极化。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了新颖的、观察者无关的引力波极化分类，揭示了此前未被注意的极化混合效应，为检验洛伦兹破缺引力理论提供了新途径。

Abstract: Gravitational wave (GW) polarizations are traditionally classified in a fixed frame ($E(2)$ classification), which does not account for how polarization patterns change under Lorentz boosts. In this work, we derive the explicit transformation laws for all six GW polarizations under longitudinal and transverse boosts. For gravity theories devoid of preferred frames, we propose a symmetry-based classification of the GW polarizations they admit. Among our key findings, we demonstrate that a propagating mode with five degrees of freedom strictly locks its longitudinal and breathing scalar amplitudes via the universal relation $A_l/A_b = -2(1-k^2/ω^2)$. For theories with a preferred frame, we analyze Bumblebee gravity and reveal that preferred-frame effects induce significant GW birefringence and observer-dependent polarization mixing. Crucially, we identify a novel vector-to-tensor polarization conversion mechanism, where vector modes in the preferred frame inevitably generate observable tensor polarizations for moving detectors, offering a new pathway to test Lorentz-violating gravity. Our framework provides a novel, observer-independent classification of GW polarizations and reveals previously unnoticed polarization mixing effects.

</details>


### [151] [Relaxation without ringdown for a compact object in modified gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00559)
*Gianmassimo Tasinato*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一种在矢量-张量理论中由各向异性应力支撑的规则致密天体，其外部与史瓦西几何无缝匹配。由于隐藏的手征对称性，该天体的奇宇称扰动表现为纯耗散的松弛极点而非振荡准正则模，在黑洞极限下松弛模式消失且外部扰动与内部退耦，揭示了黑洞行为并非通过环信号出现而是通过松弛模式消失来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越广义相对论的新强场物理，通过致密天体的动力学响应作为引力理论探针，寻找具有全新动力学特征的解析可处理模型。

Method: 构建矢量-张量理论中由矢量场支持的规则致密解，采用最小被动边界条件，匹配外部史瓦西几何且无表面层。解析计算推迟格林函数和磁化率，通过积分内部自由度推导有效膜响应。

Result: 该天体的奇宇称扰动呈现纯耗散松弛极点而非振荡模式；隐藏手征对称性使扰动问题转化为单向输运；外部区域无常规准正则模谱；在黑洞极限下松弛时间发散、极点归零、有限频率外部扰动与内部退耦。

Conclusion: 黑洞行为是通过松弛模式的消失而非环信号的出现来实现，该模型为研究黑洞极限的强场物理提供了新视角。

Abstract: Compact objects with black-hole-like exteriors may hide new strong-field physics in their interiors, making their dynamical response a sensitive probe of gravity beyond General Relativity. We present an analytically tractable, gravitationally bound compact object with a genuinely new dynamical signature: under a minimal passive boundary prescription, its exactly controlled odd-parity sector exhibits purely dissipative relaxation poles, rather than the oscillatory modes usually associated with black holes and exotic compact alternatives. The object we study is a regular, vector-supported compact solution of a vector--tensor theory, matched without any surface layer to an exterior Schwarzschild geometry. Owing to its anisotropic stress, it can violate the Buchdahl bound and be continuously connected to the black-hole compactness limit. Its unusual response follows from a hidden chiral symmetry, which turns the perturbation problem into one-way transport rather than ordinary wave propagation. The exterior region alone has no conventional quasinormal-mode spectrum; instead, the regular interior and the matching conditions break the symmetry and quantize the fluctuation spectrum. We analytically compute the retarded Green function and susceptibility, and derive an effective membrane response by integrating out the object's interior. In the black-hole limit, the relaxation times diverge, the poles collapse toward zero frequency, and finite-frequency exterior perturbations decouple from the interior. Black-hole behaviour is therefore approached through the disappearance of relaxation modes, not through the emergence of ringdown.

</details>


### [152] [Reframing of Information Geometry via Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00649)
*Tatsuaki Wada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出用对称遥平行引力（Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity）的几何框架重新表述信息几何，通过要求统计流形上曲率和挠率均为零，将信息空间的全部几何性质编码到非度量张量中。该方法区分了ξ参数化空间与θ（或η）参数化空间，类似于广义相对论与对称遥平行引力的关系，其中θ/η坐标对应于重合规范（coincident gauge）中联络系数为零的特殊坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息几何建立在黎曼几何与对偶仿射联络框架下，作者希望探索一种新的几何表述方式，即利用对称遥平行引力（曲率和挠率均为零）的数学工具来重新刻画统计流形，从而更自然地揭示信息空间的内在几何结构。

Method: 将对称遥平行引力的几何机制引入信息几何，要求统计流形上曲率和挠率全局为零，使得信息空间的全部几何性质由非度量张量承载；在此基础上区分一般ξ参数化空间与θ（或η）参数化空间，后者对应于重合规范中联络系数为零的特殊坐标系统。

Result: 证明了统计流形上的信息几何可以完全重新表述为对称遥平行引力框架下的非度量几何，其中θ（或η）坐标与ξ坐标分别对应于不同的规范选择，θ/η坐标恰好是重合规范中联络消失的特殊坐标系。

Conclusion: 信息几何可以通过对称遥平行引力的非度量几何重新建立，这为统计流形提供了一种新的几何视角，并且θ/η参数化空间与ξ参数化空间的区分在几何上等价于广义相对论与对称遥平行引力中规范选择的差异。

Abstract: Information geometry has traditionally been formulated within the framework of Riemannian geometry and dual affine connections. In this work, we reframe this foundational structure by introducing the geometric machinery of symmetric teleparallel gravity. By requiring both curvature and torsion to vanish globally on the statistical manifold, we demonstrate that the fundamental properties of the information space can be entirely encoded into the non-metricity tensor.
  This approach allows us to distinguish the general $ξ$-parameterized space from the $θ$- (or $η$-) parameterized space, mirroring the relationship between conventional general relativity and symmetric teleparallel gravity. Specifically, the $θ$- or $η$-coordinates emerge as the special coordinates in the coincident gauge, where the connection coefficients vanish.

</details>


### [153] [Spin-torsion interaction and geodesic bending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00667)
*Sagar Kumar Maity,Amitabha Lahiri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了费米子自旋在时空中产生的挠率，该挠率诱导出四费米子相互作用，进而影响恒星级致密天体（如白矮星和中子星）内部零测地线和类时测地线的弯曲。分析表明，除了传统的曲率效应外，还存在一个额外的挠率贡献，其大小取决于费米子数密度、不同手性费米子的耦合常数以及物质分布的温度。对于白矮星，挠率对零测地线弯曲的修正非常小；而在中子星等更致密的天体中，效应可能显著增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索费米子自旋诱导的时空挠率对致密恒星内部测地线弯曲的影响，弥补传统广义相对论仅考虑曲率效应的不足。

Method: 在球对称、恒定密度的引力致密费米子物质分布假设下，通过引入挠率诱导的四费米子相互作用，分析其对零测地线和类时测地线偏折的修正。

Result: 挠率对白矮星中零测地线弯曲的修正非常小（无论低质量还是高质量白矮星），但在中子星等更致密的天体中效应可能显著增大。

Conclusion: 费米子自旋诱导的时空挠率在极端致密天体（如中子星）中可能产生可观测的测地线偏折修正，而在白矮星中效应可忽略。

Abstract: The intrinsic spin of fermions induces torsion in spacetime, leading to an effective four-fermion interaction. This affects the bending of null and timelike geodesics inside a star with a spherically symmetric distribution of gravitationally dense fermionic matter of constant density. Our analysis shows an additional torsional contribution to the geodesic deflection, alongside the conventional curvature-induced effect. This change depends on the fermion number density, coupling constants of the different species of fermions with different chiralities, and the temperature of the matter distribution. We have shown that the torsion-induced corrections to null geodesic bending remains very small for both low- and high-mass white dwarfs, whereas significantly larger effects may arise in more compact astrophysical objects such as neutron stars.

</details>


### [154] [Thermodynamic-Geometric Phase Transition and Gravitational-Wave Quasinormal Modes of Schwarzschild Black Holes in $f(Q)$ Gravity: An RVB-Residue Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00670)
*Wen-Xiang Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于留数的框架，将$f(Q)$引力中Schwarzschild型黑洞的热力学几何与其引力波准正则模谱联系起来，证明两者源于修正黑洞度规的同一解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立修正引力框架下黑洞热力学几何与引力波谱之间的内在联系，揭示两者共同的解析结构基础。

Method: 基于对称遥平行引力公式，利用Robson-Villari-Biancalana方法通过逆黑洞化函数的简单极点留数给出Hawking温度；通过同一留数控制乌龟坐标的对数单值性从而进入准正则模边界条件；在扩展$f(Q)$态空间中通过修正的视界函数和有效Wald熵构建非平凡热力学Hessian矩阵。

Result: 在广义相对论Schwarzschild极限下，热容为负且有限，一维Ruppeiner几何无内在曲率奇点，无热力学相变；在$f(Q)$扩展态空间中，热力学Hessian退简并条件与热力学曲率的奇异行为重合，并反映在光子球频率、Lyapunov指数、阻尼时间和近视界单值性的偏移中。

Conclusion: 热力学几何相结构与引力波振铃是修正黑洞度规同一解析结构的两种不同投影，两者通过留数框架内在地联系起来。

Abstract: We construct a residue-based framework connecting the thermodynamic geometry of a Schwarzschild-type black hole in $f(Q)$ gravity with its gravitational-wave quasinormal-mode spectrum. The analysis is based on the symmetric teleparallel formulation of gravity, in which the gravitational field is encoded by the nonmetricity scalar $Q$ rather than by curvature or torsion. For the Schwarzschild branch, the Robson--Villari--Biancalana (RVB) method gives the Hawking temperature through the simple-pole residue of the inverse blackening function. We show explicitly that the same residue also controls the logarithmic monodromy of the tortoise coordinate near the event horizon, and therefore enters the ingoing quasinormal-mode boundary condition. In the strict general-relativistic Schwarzschild limit the heat capacity is negative and finite, the one-dimensional Ruppeiner geometry contains no intrinsic curvature singularity, and no genuine thermodynamic phase transition occurs. In the extended $f(Q)$ state space, however, the modified horizon function and the effective Wald entropy generate a non-trivial thermodynamic Hessian. Its degeneracy condition coincides with singular behavior of the thermodynamic curvature and is reflected in the quasinormal-mode spectrum through shifts of the photon-sphere frequency, Lyapunov exponent, damping time, and near-horizon monodromy. This gives a precise statement of the internal relation between thermodynamic-geometric phase structure and gravitational-wave ringdown: both are different projections of the same analytic structure of the corrected black-hole metric.

</details>


### [155] [Closed Timelike Curves from a Vacuum Traveling Wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00788)
*Semin Xavier*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个精确的真空时空模型，该模型从满足弱能量、主能量和强能量条件的正则、渐近平坦初始数据演化出闭合类时曲线。通过两个函数（谐波横向轮廓和行波模式）固定几何结构，其演化驱动时间机器核心的闭合曲线从类空变为类时。编年视界存在一个退化极限，其中其生成的闭合类光测地线具有消失的boost和光学标量，这是Killing旋量的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在满足标准能量条件的物理合理初始条件下，广义相对论中是否允许闭合类时曲线（时间机器）的自然出现，并研究其几何特性和视界结构。

Method: 通过构建精确的真空时空解，利用谐波横向轮廓和行波模式两个函数来固定爱因斯坦场方程决定的几何结构，分析闭合曲线从类空演化为类时的过程，并研究编年视界的退化极限及其Killing旋量条件。

Result: 成功构建了一个从正则、渐近平坦且满足所有标准能量条件的初始数据出发的精确真空时空，其中发展出闭合类时曲线。编年视界在退化极限下，其生成闭合类光测地线的boost和光学标量消失，满足Killing旋量条件。

Conclusion: 在广义相对论的真空解中，可以在满足标准能量条件的物理合理初始条件下自然产生时间机器结构，且编年视界的退化极限与Killing旋量相关，这为理解因果性破坏的几何机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We construct an exact vacuum spacetime that develops closed timelike curves from regular, asymptotically flat initial data respecting the weak, dominant, and strong energy conditions. Einstein's equations fix the geometry through two functions, a harmonic transverse profile and a traveling-wave mode, whose evolution drives the closed curves of the time-machine core from spacelike to timelike. The chronology horizon admits a degenerate limit in which its generating closed null geodesic has vanishing boost and optical scalars, the conditions for a Killing spinor.

</details>


### [156] [Gravitational Wave Signatures of Schwarzschild Black Hole in a Generalized Dehnen-Type $(1,4,γ)$ Dark Matter Halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00812)
*Bahromjon Shokirov,Arabboy Mirzakulov,Tursunali Xamidov,Sanjar Shaymatov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Dehnen型暗物质晕中类史瓦西黑洞周围的类时测地线运动、周期轨道及其引力波信号，发现暗物质晕参数会显著影响轨道动力学和引力波波形，其特征谱位于毫赫兹频段，可被未来空间引力波探测器观测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质分布如何修改黑洞周围试验粒子的轨道动力学，以及能否通过极端质量比旋近（EMRI）系统的引力波信号来探测暗物质晕的存在。

Method: 采用广义Dehnen型(1,4,γ)暗物质晕轮廓描述黑洞周围暗物质分布，求解类时测地线运动方程分析轨道动力学，并使用数值赝势方法（numerical kludge approach）计算轨道轨迹及对应引力波极化。

Result: 密度轮廓参数γ增大会导致MBO和ISCO半径及角动量增大、ISCO能量升高；暗物质晕参数γ、ρ_s、r_s增大会产生更大的周期轨道、更长的轨道周期和更低波形振幅；频谱主要在毫赫兹频段，部分特征应变峰值高于LISA、Taiji、TianQin等探测器灵敏度曲线。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕可能在EMRI引力波信号中留下可观测的印记，未来空间引力波探测器有望通过EMRI信号探测黑洞周围的暗物质分布。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate timelike geodesic motion, periodic orbits, and the associated gravitational-wave signals around a Schwarzschild-like black hole (BH) embedded in a generalized Dehnen-type dark matter (DM) halo. We show that the Dehnen-type $(1,4,γ)$ DM halo profile modifies test-particle dynamics, with increasing the parameter of density profile, $γ$, leading to larger marginally bound orbit (MBO) and innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) radii and angular momenta, together with a higher ISCO energy. These findings provide further insight into the role of the DM distribution in modifying the orbital dynamics, energy, and angular momentum of timelike test particles near the BH. Furthermore, we investigate the gravitational-wave signals produced by a stellar-mass compact object moving along periodic orbits around a supermassive BH embedded in a generalized Dehnen-type DM halo. Using the numerical kludge approach, we calculate the orbital trajectories and the corresponding gravitational-wave polarizations. We find that increasing the halo parameters $γ$, $ρ_s$, and $r_s$ produces larger periodic orbits, longer orbital periods, and lower waveform amplitudes. The resulting spectra lie mainly in the millihertz frequency range, while several characteristic-strain peaks lie above the sensitivity curves of future space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin. These results suggest that the surrounding DM halo may leave observable imprints on extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) gravitational-wave signals.

</details>


### [157] [Environmental effects vs. modified gravity in the LISA massive black hole binary population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00845)
*Lorenzo Copparoni,Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了LISA探测到的超大质量黑洞双星引力波信号中，天体物理环境效应与修正引力效应是否可区分。通过分析吸积盘环境（吸积和粘性迁移）与修正引力（时变牛顿常数Ġ）在同阶后牛顿展开下的简并性，结合Fisher矩阵和层次嵌套采样分析合成星表，发现即使在极端天体物理假设下（50%活跃比例、超爱丁顿吸积尾），群体后验分布仍与真空情况完全兼容。环境效应不太可能在现实天体物理场景中偏差LISA的广义相对论检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LISA观测到的超大质量黑洞双星引力波信号中，天体物理环境效应与修正引力效应是否可区分，以及环境效应是否会模仿修正引力信号导致对广义相对论检验的偏差。

Method: 采用Fisher矩阵预测结合层次嵌套采样分析，对来自天体物理动机群体模型的合成星表进行贝叶斯推断。选取环双星盘的吸积和粘性迁移作为环境通道，时变牛顿常数Ġ作为修正引力通道，三者均在相同负后牛顿阶数下由共同相位变形参数描述。

Result: 即使在天体物理极端假设下（50%活跃比例、超爱丁顿吸积尾），群体级后验分布仍与真空情况完全兼容。层次群体分析可给出活跃分数的非平凡上限和爱丁顿比分布斜率的弱下限。

Conclusion: 环境效应在现实天体物理场景中不太可能偏差LISA对广义相对论的检验，不会对修正引力信号的探测构成实质性混淆威胁。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave signals from massive black hole binaries observed by LISA can carry imprints of both the astrophysical environment of the source and possible deviations from general relativity. We investigate whether environmental effects leave a detectable imprint on the LISA binary population, and whether they can mimic modified-gravity effects with the same frequency dependence. As representative channels we adopt accretion and viscous migration in a circumbinary disk for the environmental sector, and a time-varying Newton constant $\dot G$ for the modified-gravity sector. All three effects enter the waveform at the same negative post-Newtonian order and are described, at leading order, by a common phase-deformation parameter, which makes them formally degenerate at the single-event level. Combining Fisher-matrix forecasts with a hierarchical nested-sampling analysis of synthetic catalogs from astrophysically motivated population models, we find that, even under extreme astrophysical assumptions -- an active fraction of $50\%$, together with a super-Eddington accretion tail -- the population-level posteriors remain fully compatible with vacuum. However, a hierarchical population-wide analysis may yield a non-trivial upper limit on the active fraction and a mild lower bound on the slope of the Eddington-ratio distribution. Environmental effects are therefore unlikely to bias LISA's tests of general relativity with massive black hole binaries in astrophysically realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [158] [Horizon-scale intensity and polarization images of rotating Konoplya-Zhidenko black holes with thick accretion flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01017)
*Bing-Bing Chen,Chen-Yu Yang,Guo-Ping Li,Xin-Yun Hu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Konoplya-Zhidenko旋转非Kerr黑洞在几何厚且光学薄吸积流下的阴影和偏振图像，发现厚盘模型产生的图像特征与薄盘模型显著不同，可作为探测时空几何和近吸积动力学的有效探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Konoplya-Zhidenko旋转非Kerr黑洞在厚吸积流环境下的阴影和偏振特征，并比较与薄盘模型的差异，以评估这些观测特征作为探测时空几何和近吸积动力学探针的有效性。

Method: 采用几何厚且光学薄的吸积流，通过解析弹道近似吸积流模型描述吸积流，数值模拟计算黑洞阴影和偏振图像，并分析变形参数η、自旋参数a和观测者倾角θ_o的影响。

Result: 阴影图像呈现外亮环（高阶像）和内暗区（事件视界）两个主要特征。增大η不显著改变高阶像整体形状但扩大其尺寸；增大a和θ_o增强高阶像不对称性，使左侧强度远大于右侧，与参考系拖曳和相对论多普勒效应相关。偏振图像中，高阶像区域线性偏振度远小于其他区域，偏振向量遍布整个像平面。厚盘模型产生的强度与偏振图像特征与薄盘模型显著不同。

Conclusion: 在本文框架内，观测到的强度和偏振特征可作为探测底层时空几何和近地平线吸积动力学的有效探针。

Abstract: We investigate the shadow and polarization images of a Konoplya-Zhidenko rotating non-Kerr black hole surrounded by a geometrically thick and optically thin accretion flow. The accretion flow is described by an analytical ballistic approximation accretion flow model. The numerical results show that the shadow image exhibits two main features, an outer bright ring and an inner dark region. The former corresponds to higher order images, while the latter is produced by the black hole event horizon. Increasing the deformation parameter $η$ does not significantly change the overall shape of the higher order images, but it enlarges their size. Increasing the spin parameter $a$ and the observer inclination angle $θ_o$ enhances the asymmetry of the higher order images and makes the intensity on the left side much larger than that on the right side. This behavior is associated with frame dragging and the relativistic Doppler effect. In the polarization images, the degree of linear polarization is much smaller in the higher-order image region than in other regions, and the polarization vectors extend over the whole image plane. These results indicate that the thick disk model produces features in both intensity and polarization images that differ markedly from those in thin disk models. Within the framework used in this work, the observed intensity and polarization signatures can serve as effective probes of the underlying spacetime geometry and near horizon accretion dynamics.

</details>


### [159] [Backreaction of stimulated Hawking radiation in an optical analogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01118)
*Lorenzo M. Procopio,Raul Aguero-Santacruz,David Bermudez,Ulf Leonhardt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过光纤光学模拟事件视界，首次实验证实了霍金辐射的直接产生过程，并测量了其对引力场的反作用，揭示了从场量子到霍金量子的能量转化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍金辐射连接引力、量子力学和热力学，但其产生机制（场量子如何生成霍金量子）一直未知，且天文观测几乎不可能，需要实验室模拟来揭示其物理过程。

Method: 利用光纤光学模拟事件视界，结合理论分析与实验测量，研究霍金辐射的产生过程及其反作用。

Result: 发现了不同于以往认知的简单直接过程，并成功测量了该过程对场的反作用（backreaction）。

Conclusion: 该直接过程可能同样存在于其他实验室模拟系统乃至引力场中，为理解黑洞如何辐射提供新视角。

Abstract: Hawking radiation - the emission of quantum particles at the event horizon of a black hole - connects gravity with quantum mechanics and thermodynamics; the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy has been the benchmark for potential quantum theories of gravity. But Hawking radiation has never been observed in astronomy, only in laboratory analogues and the chances of ever observing it in space are astronomically small. The energy of Hawking radiation must come from the gravitational field around the black hole, but how field quanta generate Hawking quanta has been unknown. Here we report on experimental and theoretical evidence for the process that generates Hawking radiation in a fibre-optical analogue of the event horizon. There, as in gravity, it has been believed that Hawking radiation comes from a complicated, cascaded process; here we have found a simple, direct process and measured its backreaction on the field. Our findings suggest an equally direct process for other laboratory analogues and perhaps also for gravitational fields, shedding light on how black holes might radiate.

</details>


### [160] [Preheating and oscillon formation in Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01177)
*Areef Waeming,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea,Katy Clough,Pau Figueras,Áron D. Kovács*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了早期宇宙中非线性过程产生的振荡子（oscillons）在标量-张量理论中受高阶导数修正的影响。发现虽然振荡子性质变化不大，黑洞也不普遍形成，但在大耦合情况下，形成期可能导致有效场论失效，需引入更高阶项。若振荡子存活，则趋于稳定且EFT修正可控。EFT失效由振荡子高密度区域的曲率项触发，表明忽略局部反作用和场非线性的近似方法可能遗漏重要效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究早期宇宙中非微扰过程形成的标量场振荡结构（oscillons）在高阶导数修正下的行为变化，以及有效场论描述的有效性和失效边界。

Method: 在标量-张量理论中引入领头阶高阶导数贡献，数值研究振荡子的形成与增长过程，分析EFT的有效性边界。

Result: 振荡子性质变化不显著，黑洞未普遍形成；大耦合时形成期可导致EFT失效；幸存振荡子稳定且EFT修正可控；EFT失效由高密度区域的大曲率项触发。

Conclusion: 忽略局部反作用和场非线性的近似方法可能遗漏重要效应；振荡子形成后通常稳定，但形成过程中需谨慎处理EFT截断。

Abstract: Non-perturbative processes in the early universe may create overdense structures in scalar fields like the inflaton, called oscillons. In this work, we explore whether the leading order higher derivative contributions to the scalar-tensor theory change the formation and growth of these structures, and investigate the limits in which the effective field theory (EFT) description breaks down. We find that whilst the properties of the oscillons are not significantly modified, and black holes do not generically form, for large couplings the period of formation can result in the evolution leaving the regime of validity of the EFT, at which point predictivity is lost and the next order terms in the EFT should become relevant. If the oscillons survive their formation, they tend to be stable and the EFT corrections remain bounded. The EFT breakdown is triggered by large curvature terms in the metric in the densest regions of the oscillon, meaning that approximations of such modified theories that neglect the local backreaction and non-linear dynamics of the fields may miss important effects.

</details>
